## 2026-11-06 B/G479 — Silent SW reload-token write-drop fail-closed (Source/Test candidate)

- **Authority:** protected main `423a4cbee629db3cb75c836b5286ecb376fe458c` at intake, Open PR 0; previous A/G9R #783 protected MERGED with exact-main CI/Pages SUCCESS. - **Independent negative-first CI:** #778 handled thrown sessionStorage denial, but `enforceLiveVersionHandoff` still assumed a nonthrowing `sessionStorage.setItem(RELOAD_KEY,'1')` was durable. A silently dropped token could repeatedly invoke `location.reload()` with an active SW but no controller. Source-bound synthetic VM test-only commit `15c6ebb548be8d422a6ae744be05be3e0797b10e` triggered [real Frontend Regression #42146740196 / job #127659930025](https://github.com/easonliu714/pokemon-sleep-ai-manager/actions/runs/42146740196/job/127659930025), failing `Verify G295 current watchdog lifecycle source-bound VM`: `silently dropped reload token is not durable` (actual undefined, expected false).

Missing/dropped/denied storage suppresses reload, emits existing `version_handoff_reload_guard_unavailable` blocked diagnostic and permits background SW update/normal ESM bootstrap. Existing throw-denial, successful first guarded reload, and already-controlled SW cases remain. ## 2026-11-04 B/G295 — timer-observed visibility transition fallback (Source/Test candidate)

- Exact protected intake main `3cdf729197e7b743d4a8ffe864d709a1a8d751c5`; Open PR 0; Ruleset #25813226 ACTIVE. A/G9R has no independently classifiable new post-#733 scheduled official HTML response, so `A_GATE_NO_NEW_INDEPENDENT_LIVE_SOURCE_GAP` remains; no new official facts are promoted.
- Independent code-derived #295 root cause: the watchdog suppresses timer gaps while `document.visibilityState !== 'visible'`, but does not remember that a hidden timer callback observed the page backgrounded. If Chromium omits both `visibilitychange` events, the first visible callback after a long suspended interval can satisfy the 4-second RAF/timer gap detector and falsely report a foreground main-thread stall. This is an isolated synthetic lifecycle risk, not an observed Android incident.
- Minimal read-only source fix remembers timer-observed visibility and resets watchdog clocks on observed background/foreground transitions, including explicit diagnostic provenance; preserves real foreground detection after grace, 30-frame recovery, foreign-owned storage warnings, and existing lifecycle handling. Source-bound VM and real Chromium DOM regressions cover both missed events, suppressed false alert, and subsequent real gap re-arm.
- `B_GATE=G295_TIMER_OBSERVED_VISIBILITY_FALLBACK` remains SOURCE_TEST_CANDIDATE until exact-head Draft PR CI, protected expected-head Merge Commit, and new-main exact-SHA CI/Pages. No player SQLite/IDB, Public Master/manifest, production Writer, STOP, migration, SW or app-version changes. #295 and #19 stay OPEN; 11/08 and 11/09 event-effective Android UAT not yet due; #482 remains `HOLD_OWNER_DEVICE_UNAVAILABLE / OWNER_ACTION=NONE_NOW` without Manifest request. Next A: independently classify a new official HTML response if available; otherwise choose another B Roadmap root cause.

### Stop / continue boundaries

- **現在可做**：以新手機一般重開 PWA →「備份還原」→「檢查目前資料庫」取得無破壞性持久化 readback；如 Owner 已有舊備份的 **去識別 Manifest**，僅 SHA/size/counts 比對，不要求再還原，不將個人 SQLite 交 GitHub。
- **仍需研究**：G479 Android 真實載入失敗和 startup UI elapsed label；#487 只能稱 synthetic regression、不能稱 Root Cause Fixed。
- **不可做**：清理儲存空間、卸載 PWA、推送手機私人 DB、為了湊 SHAs 強制改寫、擅自提升匿名公版 effect 為玩家數值、重開已 CLOSED Gate。
- **協作來源權威**：先 GitHub Live main + 兩份 living documents + Gate Roadmap + Issue/CI；**本地 Windows/Android checkout HEAD/dirty 未取得 => `LOCAL_HEAD_NOT_VERIFIED`**。本 Markdown 文件既有的 `2026-10-13` 原始段落為歷史稽核，不得覆蓋此新 reconciliation。

## Long-lived progress baseline governance (mandatory)

1. 每次對話／每小時任務發生實質進度更新，先確認 GitHub current main、branch、PR exact head、CI/Pages、Issue 原 DoD，**同步更新本檔及配套 UI/API/DB architecture tree**，走 branch/PR/CI/受控 merge；不得只改對話摘要。
2. 更新記錄至少保留 MAIN_SHA、SOURCE_REF、PR/ISSUE、Gate/功能 Owner、CODE、exact-head CI、Pages、Owner Android、PUBLIC_DATA_SHA、PLAYER_DB_WRITE、EVIDENCE、NEXT_GATE；未知即 NOT_VERIFIED。
3. docs/PROJECT_GATE_ROADMAP.md 是頂層 Gate authority；本檔詳列 Issue/PR 與進度，同步另一檔 UI/API/DB；不得相互矛盾。僅 Doc/CI PASS 不可替代 owner physical gate。
4. 已 CLOSED 的 Gate 不重做；真實官方活動未驗證不偽造資料；私有 SQLite、Raw、Public Master、UNKNOWN 不可被隱含寫入。

> **防漂移**：本報告的 SHA 僅代表此次 readback；後續施工前必須重新讀 GitHub current authority。本地 Windows checkout 未連線，`LOCAL_HOST_AUTHORITY=NOT_VERIFIED`。

| G9 新未來資料 only 更新 | **OPEN／REAL E2E 未驗收** | 新正式來源＋不同 SHA＋同 `.22` PWA 畫面／last-good proof 尚未完整 |
| 現有 Roadmap | `docs/PROJECT_GATE_ROADMAP.md` blob `9c47103c6db0a78696e77c49c74414dfe25827f1` | main 仍錯載 `.22 PR #471 active／Owner pending`；#472 分支已提出更正，**尚未進 main** |
| 個人 SQLite／裝置當下 | **NOT_VERIFIED** | 不宣稱遠端能看到使用者私有 DB、手機即時資料或完整 parity |

### 採用的狀態字典

- `CLOSED_OWNER`：正式實作、CI/Pages、指定 Owner Android 驗收有可追溯的完成紀錄。
- `CLOSED_GATE`：限特定子 Gate／歷史證據 PASS；不推論父 Epic 全部完成。
- `IMPLEMENTED_CI`：正式主線程式和回歸存在，尚需獨立 Owner／資料／整體端到端條件。
- `PARTIAL`：可重用骨架已存在，尚無完整產品成果。
- `REVIEW_CLOSE`：疑似 Issue／PR 遺漏核銷，必須依原 DoD 與後續證據做逐項 readback，不自動關閉。
- `OPEN`／`BLOCKED`：真正剩餘功能或無可用權威，不能憑現有按鈕／函數推論 PASS。
- `SPEC_ONLY`：Roadmap／設計文件想達成，但未發現相應完整實體。

## 5. 缺漏／風險／排序

### P0 — 防止現行主線成果誤判

1. **[NOW] #472 PR closure**：以最新 exact HEAD recheck complete required PR checks；不得跳過數量不等的檢查；合併後讀 exact-main CI+Pages，並只宣告 SYNTHETIC 子 Gate PASS。
2. **[NOW] #19真實 data-only E2E**：不同官方來源有效版次與 SHA、原 `.22` 裝置前景更新、offline last-good、玩家私有欄位保留；source/payload 衝突 fail closed。無新公告則保持 OPEN。
3. **[NOW] 舊六 PR 審核**：#434/#429–431/#359–360；不 auto merge；若明確 superseded 則留言指向新 successor／關閉分支 PR。
4. **[NOW] Roadmap 本體更新**：從已 merge main 衍生合法 branch 修補，禁止用 branch 上的文件提前宣稱 main 已更新。

### P1 — 後續功能實質缺口

- G8 #18 七日 planner 與本機存檔；WAR.1 #20/#40/#265 命名團隊持久化、對照與能量模型。
- G14 #43 ROI/sleep/coach；G15 #44 真正隔離的 scenario simulator。
- G13 #42 完整 OCR/provider/cost/empty-state 與近似去重 #58；DATA.2A #144 實際私人 75/75 closure。
- Public Master #48 未核完的資料涵蓋，#278 物種機率，#164 Evolution UNKNOWN evidence。

### P2 — Issue hygiene，不重跑已關 Gate

- #420→#295→#457 高價值證據比對；#6 僅限 no-waste 的新驗收決策。
- Issue #9/各 parent checklist 與 Canonical Roadmap 清楚劃分「功能已編碼」「CI PASS」「Owner PASS」「後續獨立缺口」，避免 checklist stale 觸發重工。
- 對 Issue 每輪固定記錄：`OWNER / CODE_REF / DATA_AUTHORITY / HEAD / CI_RUN / OWNER_UAT / BLOCKER / NEXT_PROOF / CLOSE_DECISION / LAST_VERIFIED_AT`。

## 6. 強制治理／更新流程

1. Fresh-read `main`、`version-authority.js`、Open PR exact HEAD、必要 CI+Pages、Issue 最後 comment、13 個 workflows；若本地無權存取，明示 `LOCAL_HOST_AUTHORITY=NOT_VERIFIED`。
2. 每筆新需求先找「既有 UI／函數／表格／Issue canonical owner」，找不到才能另開新 Issue／新表。
3. 對每個 Gate 分層存 `CODE_PRESENT`、`CI_VERIFIED`、`DEPLOYED`、`OWNER_VERIFIED`、`REAL_DATA_E2E`，不得跨層推論。
4. 凍結已 CLOSE 的 G5.1–5.4、G6.1、G7、G12.1、Candy Migration15/16、`.21/.22` owner 行為；只容許針對可再現回歸做最小修復。
5. 受控 **fresh main → branch → PR exact-head required checks → Ready/readback → expected-head merge → exact-main CI/Pages**。若本輪另有主施工 task 持有 branch 寫入權，本文件只能提交為交接候選，不做競態 repo 變更。
6. 嚴禁 GitHub Actions direct `contents:write`／push／commit；保持現有 13 workflow topology。官方新效果若超出 registry → REVIEW/HOLD；`pokemon_candy_multiplier` 是首次睡眠研究獲糖果，不是使用糖果培育 EXP。
7. 不得把 synthetic 測試、頁面截圖表象、資料表 DDL 或 stale Roadmap 當作真實玩家 SQLite row readback。

## 2026-11-03 B/G479 — versioned ESM failure provenance attribution (material source/test)

- **Observed source defect on main 475bcc427737fc638771feec5443d07bc3a1d3d5:** `bootstrap.js` dynamically imports `./app.js?v=<VERSION>` (and versioned critical probes), but its failure handler passed a **bare unversioned** URL to `captureModuleImportFailureProvenance`. The existing passive collector intentionally compares full Resource Timing query strings, so an available versioned HTTP 503/200 status could be silently missed and classified `UNDETERMINED`. This is a concrete diagnostic blind spot, **not** proof of the original Android transport root cause.
- **Minimal correction:** use the exact same encoded `?v=${encodeURIComponent(VERSION)}` URL in the failure provenance call. Extend the existing G479 DebugTrace source-bound VM regression to extract the production call expression, prove exact query identity, stale/unversioned exclusion, observable HTTP 503 and private query redaction. No new workflow, player SQLite/IDB, SW, app version, Public Master/manifest, migration or STOP changes.
- **Authority/closure:** #479 remains OPEN pending real Android failure response/transport evidence; this PR's exact-head CI and protected merge/new-main CI/Pages are tracked in the associated GitHub PR, not predeclared PASS here. A/G9R latest official November notices remain source-reviewed with no new deterministic promotion; #19 OPEN. G14.1 #482 `G141_CROSS_DEVICE_RESTORE_MANIFEST=HOLD_OWNER_DEVICE_UNAVAILABLE`, `OWNER_ACTION=NONE_NOW`; prior DEVICE_RESTORE_INTEGRITY_PASS and synthetic metadata CI preserved; no Owner request. #295/#6 remain separately governed; #714 merged recovery is not reimplemented.

## 2026-11-03 B/G6.2 — failed-attempt ingredient receipt write-boundary integrity

- **Source defect on main `4f1ccbbf04188d45ef55e6aeff75e3238faf4437`:** `buildRecipeAttemptFeedbackRecord()` used tolerant `normalizeRecipeAttemptIngredients()` for both historical read decoding and new write receipts. A mixed valid/invalid submitted ingredient list could silently omit invalid rows yet produce a persisted failure record; duplicate quantities could exceed JS safe integer. This is an independent receipt-integrity risk, not a new G265 CAS/ACK-loss slice.
- **Source/Test Gate:** reject an entire new write receipt unless every ingredient has a nonempty canonicalized name and a positive safe-integer numeric quantity; reject overflow after duplicate aggregation. Keep historical `decodeRecipeAttemptIngredientJson()` tolerant and preserve valid duplicate normalization. Extend the existing G6.2 source-bound contract with mixed-valid/invalid, nonnumeric, fractional, overflow, and valid duplicate fixtures. No intentionally failed meal or Owner data requested.
- **Scope:** one pure G6.2 record builder and one existing regression; no new workflow, player database/schema/migrations, Public Master/manifest, Service Worker, app version, STOP or global legacy Writer admission. PR exact-head CI, protected merge, new main CI/Pages must be independently checked before PASS. A/G9R official 11/08–11/16 notices unchanged in current source audit; `A_GATE_NO_NEW_OFFICIAL_NOTICE`, #19 remains OPEN. G14.1 #482 `G141_CROSS_DEVICE_RESTORE_MANIFEST=HOLD_OWNER_DEVICE_UNAVAILABLE` / `OWNER_ACTION=NONE_NOW`; preserve existing device integrity and synthetic CI, no Owner request. #6 remains OPEN pending full DoD and safe Android UAT.

## B/G295 — foreign-owned warning visibility-reset trace accuracy (material source/test)

- **Authority base:** main `c1e320e2de180f6a7bc7b86ed84fee94ed0a20b3`, #295 OPEN. The watchdog's `clearRecoveredHeartbeatWarning()` logged `warning_cleared=false` when a visible storage-integrity warning belonged to another subsystem, but unconditionally returned `true`. Consequently `resetHeartbeatForVisibility()` falsely logged `warning_cleared=true` during a new foreground stall followed by a visibility transition. This is an independently reproducible **diagnostic evidence misattribution**, not proof of a new physical Android freeze.
- **Minimal fix:** return the actual DOM `warningCleared` boolean, preserving foreign warning visibility, watchdog detection, native-dialog return semantics, and existing 1200ms resume grace. Extend existing source-bound VM and real Chromium lifecycle fixtures to exercise foreign-warning + new stall + visibility reset and assert `warning_cleared=false` with the warning still visible. No new workflow, player SQLite/IDB, Public Master/manifest, SW, version, migration, STOP, or legacy Writer admission.
- **Verification boundary:** exact PR-head regression, protected merge and exact-new-main CI/Pages must be read back before marking this Gate PASS. #295 remains OPEN pending its complete Android/Private Data/Safe Boot DoD; no Owner UAT notification is implied by synthetic/browser CI. A/G9R official November notices have no newly certified independent source gap in this run; #19 stays OPEN. G14.1 #482 `HOLD_OWNER_DEVICE_UNAVAILABLE` / `OWNER_ACTION=NONE_NOW`; existing device integrity and synthetic metadata evidence remain, cross-device bitwise parity unverified.

## B/G6.2 — malformed persisted feedback flags fail closed on all recommendation read sides

- **Source authority:** protected main `53c3ecb7e9542e1090574a479a6916fa139c994e` after A/G9R [#729](https://github.com/easonliu714/pokemon-sleep-ai-manager/pull/729); #6 OPEN, no competing open PR at intake. The existing `recipe_feedback_state` SQL columns are INTEGER without CHECK(0,1). `normalizeFeedbackRow()` treated any persisted value other than 1/true as **false**, so a malformed pause flag (e.g. 2) or source-review flag (e.g. -1) could silently re-admit a player-flagged recipe as a normal recommendation. This is an independently reproducible source-bound **read-side fail-open risk**, not evidence that a real player's SQLite is corrupt.
- **Minimal correction:** accept only boolean true/false and SQLite 1/0 (including text '1'/'0'); treat any other **present** flag conservatively as paused/review-required; truly absent feedback remains false. Apply once in `assets/js/recipe-attempt-feedback.js` so strategy, portfolio and unified workbench share the same normalized overlay. No SQL write, schema change, migration, Public Master, SW, app version or legacy Writer admission.
- **Regression:** existing G6.2 attempt-feedback and unified-workbench contracts add synthetic invalid/valid/absent flag cases, portfolio suppression, strategy ranking, unified weekly badge and fingerprint consistency. `B_GATE=G62_INVALID_PERSISTED_FEEDBACK_FLAG_READSIDE_FAIL_CLOSED` **SOURCE/TEST CANDIDATE** pending exact-head PR CI, Ruleset #25813226 protected merge and exact new-main CI/Pages; Android Owner UAT NOT_READY. A/G9R official 11/08–11/09 effective-event UAT remains separate; #19 OPEN. #482 remains `HOLD_OWNER_DEVICE_UNAVAILABLE`, `OWNER_ACTION=NONE_NOW`; prior synthetic integrity PASS retained and cross-device parity unverified. No Owner action requested.
